Document Control
Volume 6 is the controlled infrastructure architecture of The Eye. It governs how the 
frozen Strategic Intelligence Operating System is deployed, isolated, accelerated, 
connected, persisted, secured, observed, recovered, changed, accepted, and retired.
CONTROL VALUE
Product The Eye
Tagline The Operating System for Strategic Intelligence
Controlled document Volume 6 - Infrastructure Architecture
Edition 1.0
Issue date 1 August 2026
Status Controlled Infrastructure Architecture Baseline
Binding sources Volume 0 - Product Constitution v1.0; Volume 3 - Technical Architecture v1.0; Volume 4 - 
Engineering Specification v1.0; Volume 5 - AI Architecture v1.0
Primary audience
CTO, CIO, CISO, principal infrastructure and platform architects, SRE, security engineering, data 
and AI platform engineering, cloud and on-premise engineering, operations, procurement, 
governance, and customer technical authorities
Normative scope
Deployment profiles; topology; compute; accelerators; network; storage; messaging; runtime 
platforms; identity; policy; cryptography; sovereignty; observability; SLOs; continuity; delivery; fleet; 
economics; acceptance; governance
Downstream inheritance
Volume 7 - Data Platform; environment designs; landing zones; infrastructure modules; workload 
manifests; operational readiness plans; disaster-recovery plans; runbooks; implementation 
repositories
Normative language
SHALL, MUST, SHALL NOT, and MUST NOT are binding. MAY permits an implementation choice inside 
a frozen semantic, authority, state, isolation, failure, and evidence boundary. Requirements carry stable 
IA-CC-NNN identities. Conformance is scoped to exact artifacts, configuration, topology, customer 
domain, deployment profile, workload, consequence class, and period.
CONTROLLING RULE
Volume 0 controls constitutional meaning. Volume 3 controls product architecture. Volume 4 
controls engineering realization. Volume 5 controls AI architecture. Volume 6 controls infrastructure 
beneath those authorities. Infrastructure may host and enforce the system; it may not redefine truth, 
human authority, product semantics, or canonical ownership.

Infrastructure Architecture Charter
The Eye runs as one governed strategic intelligence system across managed cloud, 
customer cloud, customer-owned infrastructure, disconnected environments, and 
certified air gaps. Infrastructure exists to preserve semantic parity, contain failure, 
enforce trust, protect customer control, and produce operating evidence.
INFRASTRUCTURE 
COMMITMENT BINDING ARCHITECTURE CONSEQUENCE
One product across 
deployments
SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise preserve the same objects, workflows, authority, 
evidence, degraded states, replay, and lifecycle semantics.
Cell-oriented containment Tenant, domain, release, capacity, and failure scope are bounded through intelligence cells 
and explicit shared services.
State before technology Every store, queue, cache, index, checkpoint, and backup implements a declared authority, 
consistency, locality, durability, and recovery class.
Trust outside workloads Identity, policy, cryptography, capabilities, network, artifact integrity, admission, audit, and stop 
controls do not depend on application or model compliance.
Failure is designed state Partial, stale, denied, isolated, recovering, unreconciled, and terminal conditions are explicit, 
observable, and testable.
Recovery preserves meaning Failover and disaster recovery preserve tenant, time, policy, evidence, sovereignty, human 
authority, and version semantics.
Change is controlled execution Infrastructure plans, approvals, cohorts, observations, stop, rollback, effect reconciliation, and 
closure are durable evidence.
Customers retain control Hosting choice cannot silently transfer responsibility, administrator authority, key control, 
residency, incident ownership, or acceptance.
Definition of infrastructure architecture complete
• Every component, runtime unit, state class, network zone, workload, dependency, failure domain, 
control, SLO, degraded mode, recovery path, release, and owner has stable identity.
• Every deployment profile can install, operate, diagnose, secure, fail, restore, upgrade, export, and 
retire the same strategic product contract.
• Every workload runs with authenticated identity, policy, resource, placement, network, state, secret, 
artifact, telemetry, failure, and lifecycle boundaries.
• Every critical service demonstrates capacity, availability, backup, restore, disaster recovery, 
incident, change, and security evidence for its exact released scope.
• No successful process, healthy host, reachable endpoint, or available model is represented as 
strategic success when evidence, authority, state, policy, or product quality is incomplete.
CONSTITUTIONAL INHERITANCE  /  C-001  ·  C-004  ·  C-007  ·  C-008  ·  C-035  ·  C-036  ·  C-041  ·  C-049  ·  C-052

Executive Infrastructure Architecture Statement
Volume 6 defines the physical and operational system that allows The Eye to remain 
one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across hyperscale managed service, 
customer-controlled cloud, customer-owned infrastructure, disconnected sites, and 
certified air gaps.
The infrastructure is organized around isolated intelligence cells, canonical state and evidence services, 
shared control capabilities, governed compute and acceleration, explicit network and state contracts, 
externally enforced trust, and evidence-driven operations. Technology and provider selections remain 
replaceable behind stable infrastructure component, runtime unit, state class, workload, and deployment-
profile contracts. Human authority, temporal truth, provenance, uncertainty, sovereignty, correction, 
replay, and strategic meaning do not move with the hosting model.
Figure 1. The Eye infrastructure operating topology. SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise implement the same strategic 
intelligence semantics through access and integration edges, isolated intelligence cells, canonical state and evidence, shared 
control services, governed compute, network, storage, runtime, trust, observability, continuity, and human authority.
INFRASTRUCTURE 
PLANE ARCHITECTURE OBLIGATION
Deployment and topology 
plane
Realize certified SaaS, Private Cloud, On-Premise, disconnected, and air-gapped profiles through 
regions, zones, sites, cells, responsibility boundaries, and management-plane relationships.
Compute and network 
plane
Schedule CPU, accelerators, edge runtimes, service connectivity, ingress, egress, private links, 
naming, and time under isolation, capacity, and sovereignty constraints.
State and messaging plane Implement authoritative, evidence, graph, twin, index, queue, log, cache, checkpoint, backup, and 
recovery classes with declared semantics.
Platform runtime plane Host services, workflows, agents, models, retrieval, pipelines, connectors, executive experiences, 
and contracts as identity-bound governed workloads.
Trust and sovereignty plane Enforce identity, policy, cryptography, secrets, confidential processing, isolation, artifact integrity, 
detection, response, residency, and customer control.
Reliability and delivery 
plane
Measure service levels and capacity; contain failure; restore state; command incidents; govern 
change, release, fleet, economics, acceptance, and architecture.
INFRASTRUCTURE ARCHITECTURE THESIS
Infrastructure quality is not machine uptime. It is the demonstrated ability to preserve strategic 
meaning, authority, evidence, isolation, protection, continuity, customer control, and accountable 
recovery through normal operation, overload, failure, attack, disconnection, change, and retirement.

1  Infrastructure Architecture Authority and Scope
Infrastructure Architecture Authority and Scope establishes the infrastructure necessary 
to translate the frozen constitutional, technical, engineering, and AI baselines into 
deployable infrastructure without changing product meaning. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 2. Infrastructure Architecture Authority and Scope. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Translate the frozen constitutional, technical, engineering, and AI baselines into deployable 
infrastructure without changing product meaning.
Controlled state Infrastructure component identity, deployment profile, owner, lifecycle state, control evidence, and 
conformance disposition.
Enforcement boundary Infrastructure may host canonical capabilities but may not own strategic truth, policy meaning, final 
decisions, or an eleventh product layer.
Continuity rule
When a workload, platform service, or deployment cannot resolve to an approved infrastructure 
contract, the platform shall deny registration or promotion, preserve the non-conformant state, and 
route the conflict to architecture governance.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE
IA-01-001
The infrastructure realization of Infrastructure Architecture 
Authority and Scope SHALL translate the frozen constitutional, 
technical, engineering, and AI baselines into deployable 
infrastructure without changing product meaning.
System test + review
End-to-end result plus named 
customer or institutional authority, 
exact scope, rationale, conditions, 
and expiry.
IA-01-002
Every production instance SHALL declare and continuously 
reconcile infrastructure component identity, deployment profile, 
owner, lifecycle state, control evidence, and conformance 
disposition through versioned desired state, observed state, 
ownership, and audit evidence.
Fault / recovery 
exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.
IA-01-003
The implementation SHALL enforce this boundary: infrastructure 
may host canonical capabilities but may not own strategic truth, 
policy meaning, final decisions, or an eleventh product layer.
Security / 
sovereignty test
Signed control result bound to 
identity, tenant, data class, topology, 
deployment, policy, artifacts, and 
time.
IA-01-004
SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise SHALL preserve the same 
authority, state, isolation, failure, recovery, evidence, replay, and 
human-decision semantics for Infrastructure Architecture 
Authority and Scope.
Fault / recovery 
exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.

Failure semantics
FAILURE CONDITION REQUIRED BEHAVIOR
A workload, platform service, or 
deployment cannot resolve to an 
approved infrastructure contract
Deny registration or promotion, preserve the non-conformant state, and route the conflict to 
architecture governance.
Control evidence is absent, stale, or 
bound to another release
Deny promotion or enter the declared restricted state; never infer conformance from successful 
process execution.
Deployment-profile behavior diverges 
from the common contract
Suspend the affected capability in that profile until shared fixtures pass or a narrow non-semantic 
exception closes.
Assurance evidence
• A versioned manifest and runtime inventory prove the declared infrastructure component identity, 
deployment profile, owner, lifecycle state, control evidence, and conformance disposition.
• Positive and negative boundary tests prove that infrastructure may host canonical capabilities but 
may not own strategic truth, policy meaning, final decisions, or an eleventh product layer.
• Fault exercises inject a workload, platform service, or deployment cannot resolve to an approved 
infrastructure contract and verify that the platform can deny registration or promotion, preserve the 
non-conformant state, and route the conflict to architecture governance.
• Independent review accepts complete component inventory, authority trace, cross-deployment 
conformance, and signed operational acceptance for each supported deployment profile.
CHAPTER ACCEPTANCE AND INHERITANCE
Infrastructure Architecture Authority and Scope is conformant only when every IA requirement is 
implemented for the declared scope, failure states are observable and recoverable, and evidence 
resolves to the exact released configuration. Primary Volume 4 engineering anchors: Ch. 1, Ch. 4, 
Ch. 6, Ch. 7, Ch. 72. Primary Volume 5 AI anchors: Ch. 1, Ch. 2, Ch. 6, Ch. 71, Ch. 72.

2  Specification Hierarchy and Conformance
Specification Hierarchy and Conformance establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
enforce the control order from Volume 0 through infrastructure manifests, 
implementation, verification, and operational evidence. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 3. Specification Hierarchy and Conformance. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Enforce the control order from Volume 0 through infrastructure manifests, implementation, 
verification, and operational evidence.
Controlled state Normative artifact identity, authority, version, effective time, supersession, exception, and 
dependent release set.
Enforcement boundary Lower-level infrastructure specifications may add precision but may not weaken a higher authority or 
conceal semantic divergence.
Continuity rule
When two controlling artifacts conflict or an exception expires, the platform shall hold affected 
promotion, select the highest authority, expose dependent releases, and require explicit amendment 
or closure.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE

3  Infrastructure Principles and Semantic Parity
Infrastructure Principles and Semantic Parity establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
preserve one product contract across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise while 
allowing controlled physical variation. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 4. Infrastructure Principles and Semantic Parity. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Preserve one product contract across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise while allowing 
controlled physical variation.
Controlled state Deployment capability profile, semantic parity claim, permitted physical variance, test suite, and 
evidence validity.
Enforcement boundary Provider, topology, operating model, and locality may vary; authority, object, workflow, failure, audit, 
replay, and human-decision semantics may not.
Continuity rule
When a deployment profile cannot pass the common semantic conformance suite, the platform shall 
mark the capability unavailable or explicitly degraded for that profile and block claims of product 
parity.

exception closes.
Assurance evidence
• A versioned manifest and runtime inventory prove the declared deployment capability profile, 
semantic parity claim, permitted physical variance, test suite, and evidence validity.
• Positive and negative boundary tests prove that provider, topology, operating model, and locality 
may vary; authority, object, workflow, failure, audit, replay, and human-decision semantics may not.
• Fault exercises inject a deployment profile cannot pass the common semantic conformance suite 
and verify that the platform can mark the capability unavailable or explicitly degraded for that profile 
and block claims of product parity.
• Independent review accepts identical golden fixtures, parity diffs, exception records, and 
accountable sign-off for every deployment profile for each supported deployment profile.
CHAPTER ACCEPTANCE AND INHERITANCE

4  Shared Responsibility and Customer Control
Shared Responsibility and Customer Control establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
make operational ownership, customer authority, platform duties, support access, and 
evidence obligations explicit for every deployment profile. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 5. Shared Responsibility and Customer Control. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Make operational ownership, customer authority, platform duties, support access, and evidence 
obligations explicit for every deployment profile.
Controlled state Responsibility assignment, control owner, operator role, customer-held authority, access path, 
evidence producer, and escalation path.
Enforcement boundary No responsibility may be silently transferred through hosting choice, support action, managed 
service, or contractual shorthand.
Continuity rule
When an incident or control activity has no unambiguous accountable owner, the platform shall stop 
the affected operation when safe, invoke the recorded escalation path, and keep the responsibility 
gap visible until accepted.

5  Failure-Domain and Blast-Radius Doctrine
Failure-Domain and Blast-Radius Doctrine establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
bound correlated failure by tenant, intelligence domain, cell, zone, region, site, service, 
state class, and release cohort. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 6. Failure-Domain and Blast-Radius Doctrine. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Bound correlated failure by tenant, intelligence domain, cell, zone, region, site, service, state class, 
and release cohort.
Controlled state Failure-domain identity, dependency graph, isolation boundary, recovery order, maximum affected 
scope, and tested containment state.
Enforcement boundary Shared infrastructure may aggregate efficiency only where failure, data, policy, and change blast 
radius remain measurable and enforceable.
Continuity rule
When a fault crosses its declared failure domain or the actual blast radius cannot be established, 
the platform shall isolate the suspected domain, suspend unsafe automation, preserve evidence, 
and recover in dependency order under incident command.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE

containment state through versioned desired state, observed

exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state

6  Infrastructure Traceability and Documentation 
Inheritance
Infrastructure Traceability and Documentation Inheritance establishes the infrastructure 
necessary to connect every infrastructure requirement to constitutional invariants, 
logical components, engineering owners, AI workloads, manifests, tests, releases, and 
operations. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance 
while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private 
Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 7. Infrastructure Traceability and Documentation Inheritance. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Connect every infrastructure requirement to constitutional invariants, logical components, 
engineering owners, AI workloads, manifests, tests, releases, and operations.
Controlled state Requirement identity, source authority, implementation owner, component, workload, evidence, 
release, exception, and retirement linkage.
Enforcement boundary Traceability must cross document, repository, build, deployment, runtime, incident, and retirement 
boundaries without relying on prose similarity.
Continuity rule
When a production artifact has incomplete, stale, circular, or unverifiable inheritance, the platform 
shall deny promotion or mark the running artifact non-conformant, identify affected decisions and 
services, and restore a closed evidence chain.

7  System Context and Deployment Models
System Context and Deployment Models establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
define the physical system context connecting users, customer systems, external 
sources, intelligence cells, canonical state, and shared control services. It fixes 
ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one 
Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.

intelligence cells, canonical state, and shared control services.
Controlled state Deployment instance, customer domain, region or site, cell set, trust boundary, connected systems, 
and control-plane relationship.
Enforcement boundary The infrastructure context must preserve canonical layer ownership and distinguish customer, 
platform, provider, source, and external-effect boundaries.
Continuity rule
When a dependency or trust boundary is absent from the deployed context, the platform shall treat 
connectivity as denied or indeterminate, prevent implicit trust, and require context reconciliation 
before service admission.

8  SaaS Reference Architecture
SaaS Reference Architecture establishes the infrastructure necessary to operate a 
multi-region managed service with cell isolation, tenant control, governed shared 
services, and continuous upgradeability. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 9. SaaS Reference Architecture. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Operate a multi-region managed service with cell isolation, tenant control, governed shared 
services, and continuous upgradeability.
Controlled state SaaS environment, region, zone, cell, tenant placement, shared-service binding, release cohort, and 
operational state.
Enforcement boundary Multi-tenancy may share physical capacity only where identity, policy, state, telemetry, encryption, 
quotas, and failures remain tenant-scoped.
Continuity rule
When a region, cell, shared service, or tenant isolation control becomes unavailable or suspect, the 
platform shall route or contain within approved residency and consistency limits, enter explicit 
degraded mode, and preserve tenant evidence.

9  Private Cloud Reference Architecture
Private Cloud Reference Architecture establishes the infrastructure necessary to deploy 
the full product into a customer-controlled cloud boundary while preserving The Eye's 
control contracts and supportability. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 10. Private Cloud Reference Architecture. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Deploy the full product into a customer-controlled cloud boundary while preserving The Eye's control 
contracts and supportability.
Controlled state Customer cloud account or subscription, regions, network attachments, cell set, customer-managed 
dependencies, and release channel.
Enforcement boundary Customer control of cloud resources may change responsibility but not semantic behavior, evidence 
quality, upgrade discipline, or human authority.
Continuity rule
When a required customer-managed service, permission, quota, or network path violates the 
certified profile, the platform shall surface the exact dependency state, disable affected capabilities, 
and operate only within the last verified boundary.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE

contracts and supportability.

exact scope, rationale, conditions, 
and expiry.

reconcile customer cloud account or subscription, regions, 
network attachments, cell set, customer-managed dependencies, 
and release channel through versioned desired state, observed 
state, ownership, and audit evidence.

10  On-Premise Reference Architecture
On-Premise Reference Architecture establishes the infrastructure necessary to operate 
the complete platform on customer-owned infrastructure with deterministic packaging, 
local control, bounded dependencies, and recoverable lifecycle management. It fixes 
ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one 
Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 11. On-Premise Reference Architecture. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Operate the complete platform on customer-owned infrastructure with deterministic packaging, local 
control, bounded dependencies, and recoverable lifecycle management.
Controlled state Site, cluster, appliance or node set, certified dependency versions, local control services, data 
domains, and installed release state.
Enforcement boundary The installation boundary must remain operable, diagnosable, secure, upgradeable, exportable, and 
recoverable without hidden SaaS dependencies.
Continuity rule
When local infrastructure drifts beyond the certified compatibility envelope or loses vendor 
connectivity, the platform shall continue within the declared support window, block unsafe 
promotion, expose drift, and use signed offline maintenance artifacts.
Normative requirements

11  Disconnected and Air-Gapped Operations
Disconnected and Air-Gapped Operations establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
preserve bounded strategic operations where external connectivity is denied, 
intermittent, mediated, or physically separated. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, 
degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence 
Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 12. Disconnected and Air-Gapped Operations. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Preserve bounded strategic operations where external connectivity is denied, intermittent, mediated, 
or physically separated.
Controlled state Connectivity state, import and export package, transfer authority, content inspection, time basis, 
update channel, and synchronization ledger.
Enforcement boundary No runtime path may assume public DNS, external identity, cloud control, telemetry export, model 
provider, or license service inside an air gap.
Continuity rule
When trusted time, update media, transfer station, or synchronization evidence is unavailable, the 
platform shall enter the defined local continuity mode, reject unverifiable packages, and preserve 
pending exchanges for later reconciliation.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE
IA-11-001
The infrastructure realization of Disconnected and Air-Gapped 
Operations SHALL preserve bounded strategic operations where 
external connectivity is denied, intermittent, mediated, or 
physically separated.
Automated 
conformance test
Versioned fixture and signed result 
for exact manifests, artifacts, 
configuration, topology, workload, 
and environment.

Every production instance SHALL declare and continuously 
reconcile connectivity state, import and export package, transfer 
authority, content inspection, time basis, update channel, and 
synchronization ledger through versioned desired state, observed 
state, ownership, and audit evidence.
Fault / recovery 
exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.
IA-11-003
The implementation SHALL enforce this boundary: no runtime 
path may assume public DNS, external identity, cloud control, 
telemetry export, model provider, or license service inside an air 
gap.
Security / 
sovereignty test
Signed control result bound to 
identity, tenant, data class, topology, 
deployment, policy, artifacts, and 
time.
IA-11-004
SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise SHALL preserve the same 
authority, state, isolation, failure, recovery, evidence, replay, and 
human-decision semantics for Disconnected and Air-Gapped 
Operations.
Fault / recovery 
exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.

12  Regional, Zonal, Site, and Cell Topology
Regional, Zonal, Site, and Cell Topology establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
compose locations and cells into explicit availability, latency, sovereignty, capacity, and 
recovery domains. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and 
assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, 
Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 13. Regional, Zonal, Site, and Cell Topology. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Compose locations and cells into explicit availability, latency, sovereignty, capacity, and recovery 
domains.
Controlled state Topology node, parent domain, role, locality, dependencies, capacity, consistency class, health, and 
evacuation state.
Enforcement boundary Placement must satisfy residency, latency, replication, quorum, failure-domain, data-class, and 
customer-control constraints together.
Continuity rule
When topology membership, quorum, locality, or health becomes ambiguous, the platform shall 
freeze unsafe placement, prefer consistency and sovereignty over convenience, and execute the 
recorded evacuation or isolation plan.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE

13  Tenant, Domain, and Intelligence Cell Isolation
Tenant, Domain, and Intelligence Cell Isolation establishes the infrastructure necessary 
to make customer, intelligence-domain, workload, state, network, key, policy, quota, 
telemetry, and failure isolation enforceable at runtime. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 14. Tenant, Domain, and Intelligence Cell Isolation. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Make customer, intelligence-domain, workload, state, network, key, policy, quota, telemetry, and 
failure isolation enforceable at runtime.
Controlled state Tenant and domain identity, cell placement, namespace, key scope, network policy, quota, state 
binding, and isolation posture.
Enforcement boundary Shared services may receive requests only through authenticated tenant and purpose context and 
may not infer scope from network location.
Continuity rule
When tenant context is missing, conflicting, or leakage is suspected, the platform shall fail closed, 
quarantine affected paths and evidence, rotate or revoke exposed authority, and initiate cross-
tenant impact analysis.
Normative requirements

14  Shared Control Services and Management Plane
Shared Control Services and Management Plane establishes the infrastructure 
necessary to operate identity, policy, registry, configuration, keys, telemetry, release, 
licensing, and fleet control without placing customer data truth in the management 
plane. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while 
preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, 
and On-Premise.
Figure 15. Shared Control Services and Management Plane. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Operate identity, policy, registry, configuration, keys, telemetry, release, licensing, and fleet control 
without placing customer data truth in the management plane.
Controlled state Control-service identity, desired state, policy and registry versions, fleet inventory, command state, 
evidence, and operator action.
Enforcement boundary Management-plane reachability must be one-way or mediated where required and cannot become 
an unbounded data-plane backdoor.
Continuity rule
When the management plane is unavailable, compromised, partitioned, or inconsistent, the platform 
shall freeze unsafe change, continue last-known approved local control where permitted, revoke 
suspect authority, and reconcile before resuming.

15  Compute Platform Architecture
Compute Platform Architecture establishes the infrastructure necessary to provide 
portable, policy-governed compute pools for stateless, stateful, interactive, batch, 
streaming, AI, and control workloads. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 16. Compute Platform Architecture. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Provide portable, policy-governed compute pools for stateless, stateful, interactive, batch, 
streaming, AI, and control workloads.
Controlled state Compute pool, node class, architecture, placement capability, health, allocatable capacity, 
reservation, and maintenance state.
Enforcement boundary Workloads declare resource, isolation, locality, accelerator, latency, disruption, and data-class 
needs; schedulers may not guess them.
Continuity rule
When capacity, node health, scheduler state, or placement guarantees fall below the admitted 
workload contract, the platform shall shed or queue by consequence and priority, preserve state, 
and prevent uncontrolled oversubscription or silent relocation.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE

capability, health, allocatable capacity, reservation, and 
maintenance state through versioned desired state, observed 
state, ownership, and audit evidence.
Fault / recovery 
exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.

16  CPU Workload Architecture
CPU Workload Architecture establishes the infrastructure necessary to run general-
purpose services, graph and data processing, orchestration, policy, and deterministic 
analytics on certified CPU profiles. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 17. CPU Workload Architecture. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Run general-purpose services, graph and data processing, orchestration, policy, and deterministic 
analytics on certified CPU profiles.
Controlled state CPU architecture, core and memory profile, instruction-set capabilities, runtime class, reservation, 
throttling, and performance evidence.
Enforcement boundary Portable workloads must define compatible architectures and deterministic behavior; architecture-
specific optimization remains an explicit variant.
Continuity rule
When CPU saturation, memory pressure, thermal throttling, or architecture incompatibility threatens 
the workload contract, the platform shall throttle, reschedule, scale, or degrade according to priority 
while preserving deadlines, checkpoints, and observable partial state.
Normative requirements

17  GPU and Accelerator Architecture
GPU and Accelerator Architecture establishes the infrastructure necessary to govern 
scarce accelerators as isolated, attestable, schedulable, observable resources for 
training, inference, simulation, and high-performance analytics. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 18. GPU and Accelerator Architecture. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Govern scarce accelerators as isolated, attestable, schedulable, observable resources for training, 
inference, simulation, and high-performance analytics.
Controlled state Accelerator model, firmware and driver stack, partition, topology, memory, health, allocation, 
tenancy, and attestation.
Enforcement boundary Accelerator sharing must preserve memory isolation, workload identity, timing controls, supply-chain 
integrity, and bounded side channels.
Continuity rule
When device health, memory integrity, driver compatibility, partition isolation, or capacity becomes 
unsafe, the platform shall drain the device, invalidate affected work, preserve checkpoints, route 
only to an equivalent certified profile, and open hardware evidence.
Normative requirements

18  Model Serving Infrastructure
Model Serving Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure necessary to serve approved 
model artifacts through stable gateways, immutable runtime images, bounded context, 
autoscaling, and evidence-rich inference endpoints. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 19. Model Serving Infrastructure. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Serve approved model artifacts through stable gateways, immutable runtime images, bounded 
context, autoscaling, and evidence-rich inference endpoints.
Controlled state Serving deployment, model and adapter digest, runtime, hardware profile, endpoint, rollout, 
capacity, fitness, and revocation state.
Enforcement boundary Serving infrastructure may execute only registry-resolved artifacts and may not alter prompts, 
context, outputs, or safety controls outside the release manifest.
Continuity rule
When artifact resolution, serving health, calibration, capacity, or safety posture becomes invalid, the 
platform shall stop new admissions, drain or revoke the affected revision, invoke the declared 
fallback, and preserve exact inference evidence.

19  Agent and Workflow Runtime Infrastructure
Agent and Workflow Runtime Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
execute agents and durable workflows as identity-bound, resource-bounded, 
checkpointed, supervised, and recoverable runtime units. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 20. Agent and Workflow Runtime Infrastructure. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Execute agents and durable workflows as identity-bound, resource-bounded, checkpointed, 
supervised, and recoverable runtime units.
Controlled state Workflow instance, agent package, execution envelope, sandbox, capability grants, checkpoint, 
queue state, and terminal disposition.
Enforcement boundary Agent processes may request capabilities but infrastructure enforces network, file, secret, tool, 
budget, deadline, and side-effect boundaries.
Continuity rule
When a worker, supervisor, queue, checkpoint, or capability service fails during material work, the 
platform shall suspend or reassign from committed state, prevent duplicate effects, preserve 
causation, and escalate unresolved terminal state.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE
IA-19-001
The infrastructure realization of Agent and Workflow Runtime 
Infrastructure SHALL execute agents and durable workflows as 
identity-bound, resource-bounded, checkpointed, supervised, 
and recoverable runtime units.

exercise

boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.
IA-19-002
Every production instance SHALL declare and continuously 
reconcile workflow instance, agent package, execution envelope,

20  Batch and Stream Processing Infrastructure
Batch and Stream Processing Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
process bounded datasets and continuous event flows with explicit identity, lineage, 
watermarks, checkpoints, backpressure, and quality gates. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 21. Batch and Stream Processing Infrastructure. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Process bounded datasets and continuous event flows with explicit identity, lineage, watermarks, 
checkpoints, backpressure, and quality gates.
Controlled state Job or stream identity, input revisions, partition set, watermark, checkpoint, output commit, quality 
state, and replay cursor.
Enforcement boundary Processing infrastructure must separate compute completion from canonical output admission and 
preserve deterministic or declared nondeterministic behavior.
Continuity rule
When partitions lag, reorder, duplicate, corrupt, or become unavailable beyond the declared 
window, the platform shall apply backpressure, quarantine affected partitions, resume from 
committed state, and mark output incomplete or stale until reconciled.
Normative requirements

21  Edge, IoT, and Remote Collection Infrastructure
Edge, IoT, and Remote Collection Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure 
necessary to collect and process remote signals under intermittent connectivity, 
constrained power, hostile networks, device diversity, and delayed custody transfer. It 
fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving 
one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-
Premise.
Figure 22. Edge, IoT, and Remote Collection Infrastructure. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Collect and process remote signals under intermittent connectivity, constrained power, hostile 
networks, device diversity, and delayed custody transfer.
Controlled state Device identity, firmware, sensor profile, locality, clock, collection cursor, buffer, custody state, 
health, and revocation.
Enforcement boundary Edge nodes may filter or summarize only through signed policy and must retain mapping to source 
observations and transfer evidence.
Continuity rule
When device trust, clock, connectivity, local storage, or custody evidence is lost, the platform shall 
stop or quarantine collection as required, preserve buffered evidence, expose gaps, and reconcile 
after trust is restored.
Normative requirements

22  Resource Scheduling, Quotas, and Admission
Resource Scheduling, Quotas, and Admission establishes the infrastructure necessary 
to admit infrastructure work according to consequence, priority, deadline, resource fit, 
tenant entitlement, fairness, and current system risk. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 23. Resource Scheduling, Quotas, and Admission. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery,

Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Admit infrastructure work according to consequence, priority, deadline, resource fit, tenant 
entitlement, fairness, and current system risk.
Controlled state Admission decision, workload class, priority, quota, reservation, budget, preemption policy, queue 
position, and reason.
Enforcement boundary Schedulers must not use customer sensitivity, tenant identity, or strategic outcome as an 
undocumented optimization signal.
Continuity rule
When demand exceeds safe capacity or quota, fairness, deadline, and priority objectives conflict, 
the platform shall apply the declared admission hierarchy, preserve rejected and queued states, 
preempt only eligible work, and notify accountable owners.

23  Network Architecture and Segmentation
Network Architecture and Segmentation establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
partition connectivity by trust, tenant, domain, plane, environment, workload class, data 
sensitivity, and external reachability. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 24. Network Architecture and Segmentation. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Partition connectivity by trust, tenant, domain, plane, environment, workload class, data sensitivity, 
and external reachability.
Controlled state Network zone, segment, route domain, policy, attachment, flow identity, inspection state, and 
approved exception.
Enforcement boundary Network location is never sufficient identity; every permitted flow requires authenticated workload 
context and policy.
Continuity rule
When segmentation state diverges, an unknown route appears, or east-west exposure exceeds the 
declared graph, the platform shall deny or isolate suspect flows, preserve flow evidence, reconcile 
desired and actual state, and assess lateral impact.

24  Ingress, API, and Access Edge
Ingress, API, and Access Edge establishes the infrastructure necessary to terminate 
external access through authenticated, rate-governed, policy-enforced, observable, and 
resilient ingress points. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and 
assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, 
Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 25. Ingress, API, and Access Edge. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Terminate external access through authenticated, rate-governed, policy-enforced, observable, and 
resilient ingress points.
Controlled state Edge endpoint, certificate, route, API contract, identity method, policy, rate class, protection profile, 
and health.
Enforcement boundary Ingress may validate and route but cannot reinterpret canonical contracts, authorize final decisions, 
or conceal downstream partial failure.
Continuity rule
When identity, certificate, gateway, protection, or downstream route becomes unavailable or 
suspect, the platform shall fail closed for protected access, preserve idempotency and request 
evidence, and expose retriable, denied, or degraded state explicitly.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE

25  Service-to-Service Connectivity
Service-to-Service Connectivity establishes the infrastructure necessary to connect 
workloads through mutually authenticated, policy-authorized, version-aware, observable 
service identities. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and 
assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, 
Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 26. Service-to-Service Connectivity. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Connect workloads through mutually authenticated, policy-authorized, version-aware, observable 
service identities.
Controlled state Workload identity, service endpoint, trust domain, contract version, authorization decision, 
connection, and telemetry state.
Enforcement boundary Service discovery and encryption establish transport; owning services remain responsible for object, 
purpose, tenant, and consequence authorization.
Continuity rule
When identity issuance, discovery, trust bundle, policy, or service endpoint becomes inconsistent, 
the platform shall deny new protected sessions, drain affected connections where safe, use only 
certified fallbacks, and preserve causal diagnostics.
Normative requirements

26  Data Plane and Control Plane Separation
Data Plane and Control Plane Separation establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
separate customer data processing from policy, identity, configuration, release, 
telemetry, and fleet control with explicit mediation. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, 
degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence 
Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 27. Data Plane and Control Plane Separation. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Separate customer data processing from policy, identity, configuration, release, telemetry, and fleet 
control with explicit mediation.
Controlled state Plane identity, endpoint, permitted command or telemetry class, direction, authentication, retention, 
and emergency state.
Enforcement boundary Control-plane compromise must not yield unrestricted customer-data access, and data-plane 
compromise must not rewrite governing control state.
Continuity rule
When plane boundaries blur, a command lacks authority, or telemetry contains prohibited customer 
content, the platform shall block the channel, revoke affected credentials, preserve evidence, 
continue approved local policy, and initiate compromise assessment.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE
IA-26-001
The infrastructure realization of Data Plane and Control Plane 
Separation SHALL separate customer data processing from

with explicit mediation.

sovereignty test
Signed control result bound to

deployment, policy, artifacts, and 
time.

Every production instance SHALL declare and continuously 
reconcile plane identity, endpoint, permitted command or 
telemetry class, direction, authentication, retention, and 
emergency state through versioned desired state, observed 
state, ownership, and audit evidence.
Fault / recovery 
exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.
IA-26-003
The implementation SHALL enforce this boundary: control-plane 
compromise must not yield unrestricted customer-data access, 
and data-plane compromise must not rewrite governing control 
state.
System test + review
End-to-end result plus named 
customer or institutional authority, 
exact scope, rationale, conditions, 
and expiry.
IA-26-004
SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise SHALL preserve the same 
authority, state, isolation, failure, recovery, evidence, replay, and 
human-decision semantics for Data Plane and Control Plane 
Separation.
Fault / recovery 
exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.

27  DNS, Naming, Discovery, and Time
DNS, Naming, Discovery, and Time establishes the infrastructure necessary to provide 
authoritative names, service discovery, endpoint health, and trusted time as governed 
infrastructure dependencies. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior,

SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 28. DNS, Naming, Discovery, and Time. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement

assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Provide authoritative names, service discovery, endpoint health, and trusted time as governed 
infrastructure dependencies.
Controlled state Name, namespace, record version, endpoint set, health, time source, clock quality, lease, and 
provenance.
Enforcement boundary Names and clocks must be tenant-aware where required, resistant to spoofing, and reconstructible 
for historical replay.
Continuity rule
When name resolution, discovery consistency, or trusted time falls outside the declared tolerance, 
the platform shall deny freshness-sensitive or authority-changing operations, use bounded cached 
state, expose uncertainty, and reconcile histories.

28  Egress, External Integration, and Source 
Connectivity
Egress, External Integration, and Source Connectivity establishes the infrastructure 
necessary to mediate outbound connections to sources, providers, customer systems, 
and external effect endpoints through explicit purpose and data controls. It fixes 
ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one 
Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 29. Egress, External Integration, and Source Connectivity. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Mediate outbound connections to sources, providers, customer systems, and external effect 
endpoints through explicit purpose and data controls.
Controlled state Destination identity, route, purpose, data class, transfer policy, credential, rate, receipt, and 
revocation state.
Enforcement boundary No workload receives generic internet access when a registered source, provider, connector, or 
capability path can express the intended operation.
Continuity rule
When destination identity changes, content is unsafe, policy denies transfer, or the external 
endpoint is unavailable, the platform shall block or quarantine the exchange, preserve the request 
and reason, retry only inside policy, and never fabricate collection or effect success.
Normative requirements

29  Private Connectivity and Customer Networks
Private Connectivity and Customer Networks establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
join customer and platform networks through explicit routes, overlapping-address 
controls, identity mediation, capacity, and shared operations. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 30. Private Connectivity and Customer Networks. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Join customer and platform networks through explicit routes, overlapping-address controls, identity 
mediation, capacity, and shared operations.
Controlled state Connection, customer endpoint, route set, address translation, encryption, service scope, capacity, 
health, and responsibility owner.
Enforcement boundary Private connectivity changes reachability but does not create trust, bypass application authorization, 
or transfer incident ownership.
Continuity rule
When routes overlap, tunnels flap, customer controls change, or the responsibility boundary is 
disputed, the platform shall limit reachability to the last verified set, expose customer and platform 
evidence, and execute the joint restoration runbook.

necessary to engineer connectivity for measured latency, throughput, loss, jitter, 
availability, convergence, and explainable path state. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 31. Network Resilience, Performance, and Observability. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state,

Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Engineer connectivity for measured latency, throughput, loss, jitter, availability, convergence, and 
explainable path state.
Controlled state Path identity, SLO class, capacity, latency distribution, loss, dependency, health, change, and 
incident correlation.
Enforcement boundary Network optimization may not violate sovereignty, isolation, consistency, evidence capture, or 
deterministic routing requirements.
Continuity rule
When a path breaches its SLO, routing oscillates, observability gaps appear, or recovery causes 
unsafe reordering, the platform shall shift eligible traffic, constrain retries, preserve backpressure, 
expose degraded paths, and diagnose from end-to-end evidence.

31  Storage Architecture and State Classes
Storage Architecture and State Classes establishes the infrastructure necessary to map 
every infrastructure state class to an authoritative owner, consistency model, durability, 
locality, protection, retention, and recovery contract. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 32. Storage Architecture and State Classes. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Map every infrastructure state class to an authoritative owner, consistency model, durability, locality, 
protection, retention, and recovery contract.
Controlled state State-class identity, owner, store, schema, partition key, consistency, replication, retention, backup, 
recovery, and lifecycle.
Enforcement boundary Storage technology is selected behind the state contract and may not merge canonical truth, derived 
projections, cache, evidence, or temporary execution state.
Continuity rule
When state cannot be classified, ownership is ambiguous, or actual durability diverges from the 
declared class, the platform shall block new writes or admission, preserve available history, expose 
indeterminate state, and restore ownership before migration.
Normative requirements

32  Transactional and Operational Datastores
Transactional and Operational Datastores establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
provide authoritative operational persistence for identities, workflows, decisions, 
policies, registries, and other consistency-sensitive state. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 33. Transactional and Operational Datastores. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Provide authoritative operational persistence for identities, workflows, decisions, policies, registries, 
and other consistency-sensitive state.
Controlled state Database cluster, schema revision, transaction boundary, consistency level, replica set, log position, 
backup, and recovery posture.
Enforcement boundary Transactions must align with service ownership; cross-service atomicity requires a declared saga, 
outbox, or equivalent durable protocol.
Continuity rule
When quorum, transaction log, schema compatibility, replica integrity, or capacity becomes unsafe, 
the platform shall protect write correctness, fail over only inside consistency rules, preserve recovery 
points, and reconcile consumers before reopening.
Normative requirements

33  Object and Evidence Storage
Object and Evidence Storage establishes the infrastructure necessary to retain source 
artifacts, documents, media, model packages, simulation outputs, evidence bundles, 
exports, and immutable audit material. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 34. Object and Evidence Storage. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Retain source artifacts, documents, media, model packages, simulation outputs, evidence bundles, 
exports, and immutable audit material.
Controlled state Object identity, version, digest, custody, classification, locality, retention, legal hold, replication, and 
access evidence.
Enforcement boundary Object stores preserve bytes and metadata; canonical meaning, provenance, admissibility, and 
policy remain owned by the producing component.
Continuity rule
When digest mismatch, incomplete multipart write, replica loss, malware finding, or retention conflict 
occurs, the platform shall quarantine affected versions, deny decision-active use, preserve custody, 
restore from verified replicas, and propagate impact.

valid and system time, write log, index revision, consistency scope, and rebuild cursor.
• Positive and negative boundary tests prove that graph storage must preserve tenant, domain, 
temporal, provenance, policy, confidence, and correction semantics across traversal and projection.
• Fault exercises inject graph partitions diverge, indexes lag, ontology compatibility fails, or lineage 
cannot close and verify that the platform can constrain affected traversals, expose revision and 
staleness, replay authoritative changes, and rebuild projections deterministically.
• Independent review accepts temporal traversal fixtures, partition recovery, index rebuild, ontology 
migration, lineage closure, and scale benchmarks for each supported deployment profile.

Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Move commands, events, work, evidence, and notifications with declared ordering, delivery, 
durability, retry, and replay semantics.
Controlled state Topic or queue identity, schema, partition, sequence, producer, consumer group, offset, retry, dead-
letter, and retention state.
Enforcement boundary Delivery guarantees are scoped per contract; exactly-once institutional effects require idempotent 
consumers and committed effect evidence.
Continuity rule
When brokers partition, consumers lag, messages duplicate or reorder, schemas conflict, or dead-
letter volume grows unsafe, the platform shall apply backpressure, preserve durable offsets, isolate 
poison messages, prevent retry storms, and expose incomplete downstream state.

38  Cache, Temporary State, and Checkpoint 
Infrastructure
Cache, Temporary State, and Checkpoint Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure 
necessary to accelerate reads and recover execution without allowing cache, scratch, 
session, or checkpoint data to become hidden authority. It fixes ownership, state, 
boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic 
Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 39. Cache, Temporary State, and Checkpoint Infrastructure. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Accelerate reads and recover execution without allowing cache, scratch, session, or checkpoint data 
to become hidden authority.
Controlled state Cache or checkpoint identity, source revision, expiry, invalidation, encryption, locality, owner, 
recovery compatibility, and deletion state.
Enforcement boundary Temporary state must be derivable, bounded, classified, tenant-scoped, and excluded from 
canonical truth unless explicitly committed through its owner.
Continuity rule
When invalidation is missed, checkpoint compatibility fails, cache contents are suspect, or 
temporary state survives its allowed lifetime, the platform shall bypass or purge affected 
acceleration state, resume only from verified checkpoints, and make recomputation or loss explicit.
Normative requirements
ID REQUIREMENT VERIFICATION REQUIRED EVIDENCE

execution without allowing cache, scratch, session, or checkpoint 
data to become hidden authority.

invalidation, encryption, locality, owner, recovery compatibility, 
and deletion state through versioned desired state, observed 
state, ownership, and audit evidence.

exercise
Fault trace, blast radius, state 
boundary, degraded mode, recovery, 
reconciliation, user impact, and 
accountable closure.

its owner.

sovereignty test

• Positive and negative boundary tests prove that orchestration reconciles infrastructure state but 
cannot define application truth, skip service contracts, or infer safe recovery from process liveness.
• Fault exercises inject control quorum, scheduler, node fleet, image pull, admission, or reconciliation 
becomes impaired and verify that the platform can freeze unsafe change, maintain eligible running 
workloads, restore control from verified state, and reconcile before promotion.
• Independent review accepts control-plane loss, node drain, admission denial, image integrity, 
rollout and rollback, and multi-version operation tests for each supported deployment profile.
CHAPTER ACCEPTANCE AND INHERITANCE

implemented for the declared scope, failure states are observable and recoverable, and evidence 
resolves to the exact released configuration. Primary Volume 4 engineering anchors: Ch. 7, Ch. 9, 
Ch. 11, Ch. 53, Ch. 59, Ch. 61. Primary Volume 5 AI anchors: Ch. 15, Ch. 18, Ch. 30, Ch. 68, Ch.

41  Workflow Engine Infrastructure
Workflow Engine Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure necessary to host durable, 
versioned state machines, timers, tasks, compensations, human gates, and terminal 
outcomes across failures and upgrades. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded 
behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence Operating System 
across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 42. Workflow Engine Infrastructure. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement boundary, 
continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Host durable, versioned state machines, timers, tasks, compensations, human gates, and terminal 
outcomes across failures and upgrades.
Controlled state Workflow definition and instance, task lease, transition log, timer, checkpoint, compensation, gate, 
and terminal state.
Enforcement boundary Workflow engines persist orchestration state; business truth changes only through owning-service 
contracts and evidence-bearing effects.
Continuity rule
When workers duplicate, timers drift, leases expire, engine state partitions, or a definition changes 
during execution, the platform shall resume from committed transitions, suppress duplicate effects, 
pin versions, and escalate irreconcilable instances.

42  Agent Execution Sandbox Infrastructure
Agent Execution Sandbox Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
isolate agent code, tools, content, networks, files, secrets, processes, resources, and 
side effects according to consequence class. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, 
degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence 
Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 43. Agent Execution Sandbox Infrastructure. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, enforcement 
boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, and 
assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Isolate agent code, tools, content, networks, files, secrets, processes, resources, and side effects 
according to consequence class.
Controlled state Sandbox identity, agent package, runtime image, grants, mounts, network policy, resource budget, 
tool bridge, and destruction receipt.
Enforcement boundary Agent instructions cannot expand capabilities; every reachable operation is independently enforced 
by infrastructure and external policy.
Continuity rule
When sandbox escape, policy bypass, untrusted content execution, secret exposure, or cleanup 
failure is suspected, the platform shall terminate and quarantine the environment, revoke grants, 
preserve forensic state, assess reachable effects, and block the package.

Infrastructure architecture contract

structured contracts, budgets, and traceability.
Controlled state Inference request, task contract, model resolution, provider route, context manifest, budget, 
response, evaluation, and receipt.
Enforcement boundary The gateway may select approved equivalents but cannot change task meaning, authority, evidence 
obligations, or consequence controls.

When provider, route, model fitness, data policy, latency, or budget becomes invalid during 
execution, the platform shall abort, abstain, or use a declared equivalent fallback, preserve the exact 
resolution path, and never silently downgrade controls.

44  Retrieval and Context Infrastructure

assemble minimum authorized lexical, vector, graph, temporal, memory, twin, and

indeterminate, the platform shall exclude the affected material, mark context incomplete, abstain 
where necessary, and trigger refresh or correction workflows.

46  Executive Experience and Collaboration 
Infrastructure
Executive Experience and Collaboration Infrastructure establishes the infrastructure 
necessary to deliver executive operating surfaces, briefings, alerts, approvals, 
collaborative review, and decision records with high availability and explicit trust state. It 
fixes ownership, state, boundaries, degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving 
one Strategic Intelligence Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-
Premise.

enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery,

Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Deliver executive operating surfaces, briefings, alerts, approvals, collaborative review, and decision 
records with high availability and explicit trust state.
Controlled state Session, workspace, briefing package, subscription, notification, collaboration channel, approval, 
and decision-state projection.
Enforcement boundary Experience infrastructure may compose products but cannot hide provenance, uncertainty, 
staleness, dissent, policy, or human authority.
Continuity rule
When a channel, session, notification, projection, or collaboration dependency fails or becomes 
stale, the platform shall preserve decision records, show degraded and last-known state, prevent 
false acknowledgement, and provide a verified alternate path.

47  Integration Runtime and Connector Isolation
Integration Runtime and Connector Isolation establishes the infrastructure necessary to 
execute source and enterprise connectors as isolated, least-privilege, rate-governed, 
schema-aware, evidence-producing packages. It fixes ownership, state, boundaries, 
degraded behavior, and assurance while preserving one Strategic Intelligence 
Operating System across SaaS, Private Cloud, and On-Premise.
Figure 48. Integration Runtime and Connector Isolation. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Execute source and enterprise connectors as isolated, least-privilege, rate-governed, schema-
aware, evidence-producing packages.
Controlled state Connector package, source identity, credential, cursor, schema, policy, rate, network path, health, 
and revocation.
Enforcement boundary Connectors terminate external protocols and may not create canonical truth, conceal source 
limitations, or share credentials across tenants.
Continuity rule
When credential, schema, source behavior, package integrity, cursor, or content safety becomes 
invalid, the platform shall suspend the connector, quarantine content, preserve custody and cursor 
evidence, rotate authority, and require requalification.
Normative requirements

Figure 49. Platform APIs, Events, and Contract Enforcement. The diagram connects infrastructure mandate, controlled state, 
enforcement boundary, continuity rule as one controlled infrastructure contract across admission, operation, degradation, recovery, 
and assurance.
Infrastructure architecture contract
CONTRACT ELEMENT BINDING DEFINITION
Infrastructure mandate Enforce canonical envelopes, schemas, versions, errors, idempotency, ordering, and policy at every 
infrastructure-mediated contract boundary.
Controlled state Contract identity, schema, compatibility range, endpoint or topic, policy, conformance fixtures, 
consumer set, and retirement state.
Enforcement boundary Gateways, brokers, runtimes, and SDKs enforce contracts consistently but cannot invent application 
success or suppress explicit failure states.
Continuity rule
When schema, version, policy, ordering, or consumer compatibility is violated, the platform shall 
reject or quarantine the exchange, preserve causation and payload evidence, and require 
compatible replay or migration.
